# Chinese 04 — Band 4-oriented extended communication

This source contains exactly **1,000** standard spoken Chinese (Putonghua) production cards in Simplified Chinese: `zh-04-0001`–`zh-04-1000`, deck `deck:zh-Hans-CN:04`. Each ID has one synchronized mapping below. All roles are **P**. A row's `accepted` status means acceptance through the EXECUTOR's editorial gates; it does not claim ORCHESTRATOR acceptance or a merge.

The [Chinese plan](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/3) and [pinned guide §6.5](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#65-upper-intermediate-operation) supply functional orientation. Section 6.5 consolidates earlier grammar under greater discourse complexity: pronunciation/prosody, accurate grammar, narration, description, discussion, argument, source comparison and formal/informal adjustment. It does **not** provide an exhaustive official Band 4 grammar or vocabulary inventory. The seven allocations and selected extensions here are authored curriculum decisions, not an invented official item list, HSK list or proficiency equivalence.

## Authored allocation and substantive coverage

| Block | IDs (zh-04-) | Cards | Actual functional emphasis |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| A | 0001–0190 | 190 | Adult-life lexical expansion and bounded evaluation; new lexical counterparts and focused senses, with support for service, social, work/study, community and cultural contexts. |
| B | 0191–0340 | 150 | Narration and description through actual result, capability, state, direction, participants and temporal relations; known systems are extended through new collocations and contrasts. |
| C | 0341–0480 | 140 | Discussion, argument, qualification, inference, stance and topic management with actual propositions and recoverable reference inside the front. |
| D | 0481–0630 | 150 | Original short sources, quotations, summaries and retellings are actually supplied; comparisons preserve actors, time, units, quantifiers, uncertainty and source voice. |
| E | 0631–0760 | 130 | Recipient-sensitive formal/conversational wording with actual relevant versions, locally stated roles/authority, requests, correspondence, courtesy and status distinctions. |
| F | 0761–0880 | 120 | Contextual whole-word readings, weak syllables, segmentation and text-grounded interaction; 0761–0860 focus on 100 contextual word/collocation reading objectives and 0861–0880 on 20 contextual interaction/repair objectives, including lexical labels for speech acts. |
| G | 0881–1000 | 120 | Lexical extensions in ordinary services, collaboration, relationships, culture, accessible nonfiction and personal development, with bounded claims and concrete contexts. |

Every hidden span remains a focused word, sense, collocation, functional component or conventional expression. A changed scene, larger visible sentence or synonym in the explanation is not counted as a new hidden objective. Existing grammar and familiar concepts may support new whole lexical retrieval; the mapping identifies the actual prior contribution rather than claiming an entire system first appears here. Prerequisite IDs refer only to actual accepted 00–03 records or earlier 04 records. Supporting words are visible/glossed support, not additional hidden cards.

The fronts use ordinary adult situations across work/study, services, shared activities, relationships, culture and accessible nonfiction. Source-comparison materials are original short texts written for these cards, not imported quotations, real institutional rules or verified real-world events. Positive syntheses appear alongside warranted conflict, scope and information-gap cases. Formal wording preserves actual content, force, certainty and commitment; it is not treated as universally preferable or region-exclusive. Textual interaction supplies actual prior turns/referents and contrasts; it does not claim heard or performed prosody.

The important contrasts have actual hidden retrieval: achieved agreement versus later payment (`zh-04-0193`); emotional noncontinuation versus decoding ability (`zh-04-0247`); approach versus contact (`zh-04-0296`); creation by drilling versus bodily passage (`zh-04-0311`); inference versus confirmed location (`zh-04-0379`); bounded dimensional completeness versus overall quality (`zh-04-0437`); and source/relay independence, pending decisions and receipt acknowledgment in D/E below. These consolidate known grammatical systems through new collocations and precise discourse scope.

F's whole-word contrasts include `zh-04-0761`/`zh-04-0762` diào/tiáo, `zh-04-0768`/`zh-04-0769` háng/xíng, `zh-04-0781`/`zh-04-0783` qǔ/qū, `zh-04-0786`/`zh-04-0787` whole-word zhuǎn/zhuàn, `zh-04-0796`/`zh-04-0797` cēn/cān, `zh-04-0798`/`zh-04-0800` chù/chǔ, and `zh-04-0823`/`zh-04-0824` noun jiào versus teaching-result jiāo. Neutral syllables and lexical segmentation are retrieved in actual words such as `zh-04-0782` 曲子 and `zh-04-0832` 数落. Interaction and clarification in 0861–0880 supply actual earlier turns, intended referents and repairs; they do not infer audible prominence from punctuation alone. These are contextual lexical/discourse objectives, not a second character-default inventory.

## Functional reconciliation with actual examples

| Guide function | Actual source examples and focused units |
| --- | --- |
| Grammatical accuracy under discourse complexity | `zh-04-0193` 谈妥; `zh-04-0247` 看不下去 |
| Narration/description | `zh-04-0296` 靠拢; `zh-04-0311` 钻出 |
| Discussion/argument | `zh-04-0379` 多半; `zh-04-0437` 全面 |
| Source comparison | `zh-04-0502` 结语; `zh-04-0629` 追溯 |
| Formal/conversational adjustment | `zh-04-0636` 征求; `zh-04-0738` 收悉 |
| Contextual word reading | `zh-04-0786` 旋转; `zh-04-0823` 教学 |
| Interaction/repair | `zh-04-0868` 我说的是; `zh-04-0875` 跑题 |
| Everyday/intellectual extension | `zh-04-0939` 改编; `zh-04-0978` 规律 |

Progression is grounded in complete relevant actual predecessor/current records and complete hidden inventories, rather than generic stage labels. Examples include numerical approximation after spatial 近; knowledge-based 所知 after 所说; whole 书面语 after the written-medium adjective 书面; standalone 确切 after 确切地说; the letter-heading noun 抬头 after the physical verb; and attention-getting transitive 招呼 after whole greeting phrase 打招呼. These do not claim the underlying concepts or character readings are newly introduced. The compact written 须/需 forms likewise build on known necessity expressions and visible uses.

The source comparisons distinguish missing documentation from absent events, receipt from dispatch, intention from fulfillment, a subset from a closed whole, and one original source from its repeated relays. For example, `zh-04-0502` supplies two walks, one per route, rather than inventing paired trials; `zh-04-0518` compares walking time rather than inferring distance; `zh-04-0544` preserves an inclusive cutoff; `zh-04-0626` limits observations to Wednesday rather than disproving an undated later report; `zh-04-0629` traces two relays to one original record. Register examples retain locally stated decision authority and uncertainty, including consultation in `zh-04-0636` and receipt acknowledgment in `zh-04-0738`.

Advanced idiomatic density, specialized professional discourse, advanced literary work and Traditional-character recognition remain later-plan development. Ordinary formal or abstract words here are used with accessible supplied contexts; no advanced domain expertise, standardized level attainment or speaking/listening performance is certified.

## Cumulative 00–04 accounting and operational inventories

Accepted 00–03 contribute 200+400+600+800=2,000 cards/mappings. This 04 source adds 1,000, giving **3,000 Chinese cards and 3,000 mappings** through 04. These are card counts, not distinct words, semantic senses or proficiency scores. Accepted Spanish 01–07, accepted Chinese 00–03, all nine language guides, AGENTS.md and .gitignore are preservation files; the 33-file final raw-byte comparison and unchanged Spanish fixture evidence are reported in the PR after the one final pass.

| Reproducible measure | Stage 04 | Cumulative 00–04 |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Cards | 1000 | 3000 |
| Mappings | 1000 | 3000 |
| Distinct exact cloze strings | 999 | 2754 |
| Distinct exact lemma strings | 998 | 2750 |
| Distinct operational (lemma, English meaning) string pairs | 1000 | 2999 |
| Unique direct-target Han code points | 806 | 1336 |
| Unique complete-front Han code points | 1411 | 1623 |
| Unique visible-context Han code points | 1234 | 1384 |
| Unique incidental-only Han code points | 605 | 287 |

Exact NFC source strings; operational pair=(lemma,meaning). Han is unique code points matching U+3400–U+4DBF or U+4E00–U+9FFF. Direct=union of actual cloze Han; sentence=union of complete sentence Han; visible context=union after removing each sentence's own single cloze occurrence, so visible can overlap global direct; incidental-only=sentence minus global direct. These are reproducible string/code-point inventories, not tokenization, semantic sense counts, exhaustive vocabulary, recognized/handwritten mastery, listening or proficiency evidence.

The guide's Band 4 working landmarks are approximately 1,200–1,500 recognized Han characters, 3,200–4,300 words/phrases and 400–500 handwritten characters, including its stated overlap with coverage elsewhere associated with Band 5. They are curricular reference points. Exact source strings and code-point unions do not establish recognition, handwriting, exhaustive word/phrase coverage, speaking or listening attainment. The guide supplies no exhaustive Band 4 item list against which to certify lexical completeness. Visible support and incidental Han are reported separately from direct retrieval; they are not silently promoted into tested vocabulary or mastered characters. Differences between these operational inventories and the guide's learning landmarks remain explicit limits of this text-only evidence.

## Reading and review basis

Every target has a contextual tone-marked Pinyin choice. Whole-word reading, neutral syllables, segmentation and supported variants are reviewed lexically; no automatic character-default converter or blanket full/weak-tone rule determines a correction. Citation 一/不 are retained unless a specifically supported lexical weak form is selected; connected-speech changes are labeled separately where given. Primary-source choices and access limits, including indexed-only, failed-body and gated-detail cases, are retained in the PR evidence. No dictionary example is copied into these original fronts.

All 25 fresh author/editor pairs and the one exhaustive final pass over 0001–0200, 0201–0300, 0301–0400, 0401–0500, 0501–0600, 0601–0700, 0701–0800, 0801–0900, 0901–1000 are complete. EXECUTOR read every complete output and adjudicated every finding/limitation; verified final corrections were applied together, followed by affected-record/dependent-mapping inspection and the completion checks reported in the draft PR. The final pass received all ten actual mapping columns and the complete reconciliation, with every assigned field value and optional-field presence preserved. Actual process counts, findings, capacity exceptions, focused/consolidated validation, exact-head CI and final source/coverage SHA-256 are reported in the PR.
